root@js2-label-studio:~# systemctl stop packagekit.service
root@js2-label-studio:~# systemctl disable packagekit.service
The unit files have no installation config (WantedBy=, RequiredBy=, UpheldBy=,
Also=, or Alias= settings in the [Install] section, and DefaultInstance= for
template units). This means they are not meant to be enabled or disabled using systemctl.
 
Possible reasons for having these kinds of units are:
* A unit may be statically enabled by being symlinked from another unit's
  .wants/, .requires/, or .upholds/ directory.
* A unit's purpose may be to act as a helper for some other unit which has
  a requirement dependency on it.
* A unit may be started when needed via activation (socket, path, timer,
  D-Bus, udev, scripted systemctl call, ...).
* In case of template units, the unit is meant to be enabled with some
  instance name specified.

```
(label-studio) hpcgpt@js2-label-studio:~$ mkdir -p ~/.config/systemd/user
(label-studio) hpcgpt@js2-label-studio:~$ cd .config/systemd/user/
```

```
(label-studio) hpcgpt@js2-label-studio:~/.config/systemd/user$ vi label-studio.service
(label-studio) hpcgpt@js2-label-studio:~/.config/systemd/user$ cat label-studio.service
[Unit]
Description=Label Studio Annotation Platform
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
Environment=LABEL_STUDIO_DISABLE_SIGNUP_WITHOUT_LINK=true
Environment=SSRF_PROTECTION_ENABLED=true
Environment=COLLECT_ANALYTICS=false
ExecStart=%h/miniforge3/envs/label-studio/bin/label-studio start --internal-host localhost --port 8080 --no-browser
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=5

[Install]
WantedBy=default.target

(label-studio) hpcgpt@js2-label-studio:~/.config/systemd/user$
```

```
(label-studio) hpcgpt@js2-label-studio:~/.config/systemd/user$ cd ~/
(label-studio) hpcgpt@js2-label-studio:~$ exit
exit
$ exit
root@js2-label-studio:~# loginctl enable-linger hpcgpt
root@js2-label-studio:~#
```
